# SECTION 0 — YOUR ROLE

You are a senior game technical director, engine programmer, gameplay programmer,
level designer, and producer combined. You have a terminal, a code editor, and an
empty git repository. Your mission is to build, over a sequence of working
milestones, a complete, original, genuinely fun open-world action game for
Windows PC and deliver it as a zip containing a working `DividedHorizon.exe`.

You are not writing a game design document. You are not prototyping one mechanic.
You are SHIPPING A GAME — every milestone ends with a playable build. The final
delivery must be downloadable, double-clickable, and enjoyable with zero setup.

# SECTION 1 — HONESTY CONTRACT (NON-NEGOTIABLE, READ FIRST)

1.1 You CANNOT recreate Far Cry 3 or GTA V asset-for-asset. Those games are the
    product of hundreds of artists, years of mocap and photography, and ~$100M+
    production budgets. Their photoreal art cannot be conjured from code. Do not
    pretend otherwise at any point.

1.2 Therefore this project is ORIGINAL WORK **inspired by the gameplay DNA** of
    those titles (see Section 2). "AAA-feeling gameplay, stylized visuals" is the
    deal. Stylized (low-poly / clean / cel-shaded) graphics, designed with strong
    art direction, will look GENUINELY GOOD and run EVERYWHERE. That is a win.

1.3 "Perfect" is defined in Section 18 (Acceptance Suite). It means: crash-free,
    stable 60 FPS on low-end hardware, complete core loops, working .exe, coherent
    art style, fun traversal and combat. It does NOT mean photorealistic.

1.4 At every milestone report you MUST state: (a) what is done, (b) what is stubbed,
    (c) what deviates from this spec and why. Never fabricate completion. If you
    cannot do something, degrade gracefully per Section 19 and SAY SO.

1.5 Never pad file size with garbage to hit 10 GB. See Section 9.

# SECTION 2 — LEGAL CONSTITUTION

2.1 ABSOLUTELY NO copyrighted material: no Rockstar, Ubisoft, Marvel, or Disney
    names, characters, logos, map layouts, dialogue, music, models, or textures.
    No "Los Santos", no "Vaas", no "Rook Islands", no parody names close enough
    to be confused with them.

2.2 Game MECHANICS are not copyrightable and you WILL implement mechanics in the
    tradition of open-world action games: outpost capture, wanted levels, radio
    stations in cars, towers/vantage points, hunting, crafting, stealth tagging,
    vehicle theft, territory control. Implement them originally.

2.3 All assets must be: (a) procedurally generated by your code, (b) CC0/CC-BY
    licensed packs from Section 13, (c) self-authored geometry/shaders/textures,
    or (d) clearly-marked temporary placeholders you list in the report.

2.4 Any text, radio DJ patter, mission dialogue, and story is authored originally
    by you in this document's spirit. Villain and hero names below are yours to use.

2.5 The product name is DIVIDED HORIZON (working title). Studio credit line:
    "Built by AI under human direction." No false human authorship claims.

# SECTION 3 — PRODUCT VISION & PILLARS

VISION: One game, two worlds, one hero.
- ACT I: a tropical ISLAND you liberate Far-Cry-style (infiltrate outposts, free
  the region from a cult, hunt wildlife, climb vantage points to clear the map).
- ACT II: a crime CITY where you rise GTA-style (steal cars, run jobs, survive a
  wanted system, build reputation with factions, buy safehouses and gear).
- ACT III: the island calls you back for the finale — full liberation war.

PILLARS (every design decision must serve at least one):
P1 TRAVERSAL IS JOY — moving through the world (on foot, in vehicles, by zipline,
   by wingsuit-lite glide) must feel good second-to-second.
P2 PLAYER AUTHORITY — multiple valid approaches: guns blazing, pure stealth,
   environmental kills, vehicular chaos. The player plans; the game answers.
P3 LIVING BACKDROP — civilians, traffic, wildlife, patrols, radio audio. The
   world must never feel like an empty level editor.
P4 RUNS ON ANYTHING — 60 FPS on integrated graphics at 900p is a launch
   requirement, not a patch.
P5 ALWAYS PLAYABLE — at no commit is the build allowed to be broken.

TARGET PLAYER FANTASY: "I am a capable lone operator in a beautiful hostile
paradise and a neon sprawl — my tools, my approach, my consequences."

# SECTION 4 — WORLD SPEC

4.1 MAP A — ISLA SOMBRA (tropical island, ~2.5 x 2.5 km playable)
   Biomes: beach & docks, jungle interior, mountain ridge, ruined sugar mills,
   cult-controlled villages, river network with jumpable falls.
   Content budget: 12 outposts (escalating difficulty), 4 vantage towers
   ("Signal Masts" — climbing reveals map fog), 3 strongholds (multi-stage),
   8 hunting grounds (wildlife: boar, capybara-like "chanchos", exotic birds),
   4 cavern/interior spaces, 20 hidden caches.
   Landmarks must be visible from distance for navigation (tower silhouette,
   lighthouse, mill chimney) — landmark-led level design.

4.2 MAP B — MERIDIAN CITY (urban, ~3 x 3 km with countryside fringe)
   Districts: Old Harbor (warehouses, smuggling), Downtown (glass towers,
   bank), Solada Beach (tourist strip, marina), Favela-esque "Alto Barro"
   hillside slums (vertical, tight alleys), industrial "La Herrería" railyard.
   Content budget: ~120 city blocks procedurally laid out + 30 hand-tuned
   hero blocks (bank, police HQ, mansion, stadium, mall), highway loop,
   2 bridges to island ferry link, airport strip.
   Life: traffic splines with signals, pedestrian sidewalk graph, day/night
   cycle (real clock-driven lighting + neon at night), 3 radio stations.

4.3 MAP STRUCTURE RULES
   - One unified streaming world per act (no loading doors inside an act).
   - Fast travel only between unlocked safehouses/vantage points.
   - Minimap + full map screen with fog-of-war on island, open on city after
     first mission.

# SECTION 5 — CORE LOOP

ISLAND LOOP: Scout from mast → tag enemies (binoculars) → plan approach →
infiltrate outpost (stealth or loud) → capture (raise flag, 30s hold) →
region unlocks (shop discount, fast travel, side content) → wildlife/resources
feed crafting → next outpost.

CITY LOOP: Pick a job (phone/map board) → prep (vehicle, weapons, outfit) →
execute (chase, heist-lite, hit, race, delivery) → heat rises or falls →
launder money → buy gear/safehouse/cosmetics → reputation unlocks bigger jobs.

META LOOP: Story missions unlock per act progression; skills unlock via
challenges (10 headshots, 5 stealth captures, etc.); the map fills in; the
finale opens at reputation/story thresholds.

# SECTION 6 — GAMEPLAY SYSTEMS CATALOG (BUILD THESE)

6.1 PLAYER MOVEMENT (feel is PRIORITY #1)
   - Walk/run/sprint with acceleration curves; crouch; slide down slopes.
   - Jump + mantle/vault (1.2 m step, 2.2 m vault, 4 m ledge grab).
   - Swim on surface + dive (breath meter); ladder climb; ziplines (island).
   - Wingsuit-lite glide from high falls (island skill unlock) — lift/drag model.
   - Fall damage with roll-to-cancel window.
   - Camera: 60 FPS game feel, coyote time 120 ms, jump buffer 150 ms.

6.2 COMBAT
   - Weapons: semi-auto pistol, SMG, assault rifle, shotgun, sniper (scoped),
     bow (silent), thrown (molotov, grenade, smoke). Slots: 2 primaries + sidearm
     + 2 throwables. Loot ammo from enemies/crates.
   - Ballistics: hitscan for guns with tracer + impact FX; projectile for bow
     and throwables. Damage zones: head x2.5, torso x1.0, limb x0.7.
   - Recoil patterns per weapon; hipfire vs ADS (sensitivity scale).
   - Enemy feedback: hit react anim, damage numbers optional (settings),
     killcam-lite slowmo on last kill of an encounter (toggleable).

6.3 STEALTH SYSTEM
   - Detection meter per enemy (suspicion → search → alert → combat).
   - Noise model: footsteps by surface, silenced vs unsilenced, bodies alert.
   - Tagging (binoculars/drone-lite): tagged enemies visible through walls at
     low opacity for 60 s.
   - Takedowns (front/back, ledge, from water), body hiding (bushes/containers).
   - Last-known-position: enemies investigate where they lost you.

6.4 ENEMY AI (behavior tree / state machine hybrid)
   States: PATROL → SUSPICIOUS → SEARCH → COMBAT → FLEE(suppress) → DEAD.
   - Patrol graphs with wait/look/phone-idle variety; vision cone + hearing.
   - Combat: cover points, flanking routes, grenade flush-outs at squad size>=3,
     reinforcements from nearest garrison (max 2 waves), morale break for
     outmatched grunts (island cultists fight to death; city gangers flee).
   - Factions: LA COSECHA (cult, island), CARTEL DEL FARO (city traffickers),
     MERIDIAN PD (city law). Hostility matrix per faction.

6.5 WANTED / HEAT SYSTEM (city) — "REPUTAZIONE"
   0-5 stars. Witnesses call it in; cameras in downtown add heat passively.
   Response ladder: 0 idle · 1 patrol cars seek · 2 cruisers + roadblocks ·
   3 SWAT vans + SMG units · 4 helicopter spotlight + spike strips ·
   5 armored units + shoot-on-sight everywhere.
   Escape: break line of sight + distance + 20 s timer (cone on minimap shows
   search zone). Bribe/launder at "El Banco Menor" after cooldown.
   Island equivalent: cult ALERT LEVEL per region (garrisons reinforce nearby
   outposts after loud attacks; stealth keeps alert low).

6.6 VEHICLES
   Classes: compact, muscle car, van, pickup, taxi (stealable), police cruiser
   (stealable = +2 heat), motorcycle, boat (island), dinghy, jet-ski-lite.
   - Arcade handling model (grip/drift/boost-by-downforce, NO sim physics).
   - Damage model: deformation proxies + smoke/fire at thresholds, explode.
   - Radio: 3 stations (Section 14) with working volume knob logic.
   - Traffic AI: spline following, gap acceptance, stop at lights, panic flee
     at gunfire (scatter impulse), parked car population at night near venues.
   - Enter/exit/steal animation states (carjack with driver pull-out).

6.7 WORLD LIFE & SYSTEMS
   - Day/night cycle 24 min real-time default (slider in settings).
   - Weather: clear → overcast → rain (island monsoon bursts) with wet-surface
     shader darken + particle rain + thunder audio.
   - Wildlife island: wandering herbivores, bird flocks (boids-lite), fish.
   - Civilians city: sidewalk graphs, panic states, 911 flee-to-cover.
   - Economy: two currencies (ISLAND: contraband bars; CITY: dollars). Shops,
     black-market weapons locker, safehouse stash, outfit changes.

6.8 PROGRESSION & SKILLS
   Three trees: SHADOW (stealth), FURY (combat), GHOST (survival/mobility).
   Skill points from levels (XP from all activities) + challenge completions.
   24 skills total (8/tree), e.g., "Double Takedown", "Hold Breath x2",
   "Glider Boost", "Scavenge x2", "Fast Search", "Hotwire".
   Crafting (island): health kits, molotovs, special arrows from hunted hides.

6.9 MISSIONS & STORY (original — Section 7)
   8 story missions + 3 finale missions = 11 total, each 10–20 min first play.
   Mission structure: cutscene-lite (camera pans + text cards + TTS voice lines),
   objectives with waypoint UI, checkpoints every stage, fail states with retry.

6.10 SIDE CONTENT
   Island: 12 outpost liberations, 3 stronghold assaults, 8 relic hunts
   (puzzle-lite platforming), 6 delivery races (vehicle/timer), 15 collectibles
   ("Corridas de la Suerte" cards with lore).
   City: 10 odd jobs (chase/hit/delivery/getaway), 5 street races, 6 robbery
   mini-missions (mini-games: hack panel timed-QTE-lite, drill timer, loot),
   10 collectibles (graffiti tags — walk up, snap photo for XP).
   Total target: 8–12 hours first playthrough.

6.11 UX SYSTEMS
   - Main menu: Continue / New Game / Load / Settings / Extras / Quit.
   - Settings: display (fullscreen, resolution, vsync, quality preset Low-Ultra,
     FOV slider 70–110), audio (master/music/SFX/voice sliders), gameplay
     (aim assist strength, difficulty Explorer/Normal/Hardcore, hints toggle),
     controls (full rebind, gamepad auto-detect with glyphs).
   - HUD: health + armor segments, ammo, minimap, compass optional, objective
     tracker, stealth eye indicator, heat stars (city)/alert meter (island).
   - Pause menu actually pauses (including world audio duck).
   - Save: 3 slots + autosave on mission stage/outpost capture/travel; JSON saves
     in user:// (never in install dir).

# SECTION 7 — STORY OUTLINE (ORIGINAL)

TITLE: DIVIDED HORIZON
PROTAGONIST: REN VOSS — ex-maritime interdiction specialist, dishonorably
discharged after refusing a corrupt order. Quiet, competent, dry humor.

ACT I — THE ISLAND (Map A)
   Ren's estranged brother left a voicemail: "If this reaches you — don't come
   to Isla Sombra." So Ren comes. The island is strangled by LA COSECHA, a
   cult-run narco-theocracy led by EL SERENO (soft-voiced former priest who
   never raises his voice — menace by calm, NOT a Vaas copy). Ren discovers
   his brother stole the cartel's ledger and hid it before vanishing. To find
   him, Ren must cripple the cult's outposts to reach the leader's stronghold.

ACT II — THE CITY (Map B)
   The ledger names MERIDIAN CITY buyers: the GILT KINGS (old-money syndicate
   in Downtown) and the DOCKSIDE UNION (harbor smugglers). Ren sails to
   Meridian to burn the network from the wallet up: jobs, heists-lite, a
   corrupt deputy mayor storyline. Twist: MERIDIAN PD's "Task Force Limpio"
   is the cartel's cleaning crew — the wanted system becomes narratively real.

ACT III — RETURN TO ISLA SOMBRA
   The brother is alive, held as leverage. El Sereno offers a trade at the
   lighthouse during a monsoon. Full liberation war: every outpost reclaimed
   returns as an ally garrison in the finale. Two endings (free your brother
   and sink the network / take the ledger's fortune and walk away) chosen
   by final interaction.

MISSION LIST (11): [1] Landing (tutorial island, silent pistol + first outpost
lite) · [2] Signal (first mast) · [3] The Ledger's Trail · [4] Sermon (first
El Sereno encounter, escape sequence) · [5] Crossing (travel to city) ·
[6] Gilt Invitation (infiltrate bank job) · [7] Union Dues (dock heist) ·
[8] Limpio (survive full 5-star setpiece) · [9] Broadcast (hijack TV station
to expose ledger) · [10] The Trade (return, night, monsoon) · [11] High
Lighthouse (finale — defend/assault with ally garrisons, choice ending).

# SECTION 8 — CHARACTER SPECS (for placeholder-safe authoring)

REN VOSS: stubby stylized character (capsule+simple mesh is acceptable through
M6; upgrade to rigged glTF humanoid with Mixamo anims by M7). Outfits: default
field gear, scuba-lite, tuxedo-lite (mission 6).
EL SERENO: white linen, cane, calm posture; 4 cinematics (camera+text+TTS).
SUPPORT: MARISOL (dock mechanic, quest giver), "HANDS" (city fence),
BROTHER TOBIAS (voicemail recordings as collectible audio logs).
Enemies: 3 archetypes/faction (grunt, shotgun bruiser, officer who buffs
nearby morale). Distinguish silhouettes per faction at 50 m.

# SECTION 9 — CONTENT BUDGET TOWARD ~10 GB (HONEST SIZING)

This game targets ~10 GB INSTALLED. Size must come from real content:
   ── Base package ......................... ~3.5 GB
      (2 maps geo+textures, UI, SFX, music, code)
   ── HD TEXTURE PACK (optional folder) .... ~5.0 GB
      (4K material sets for terrain/city/vehicles — self-generated
       procedural textures upscaled, mip-correct; toggle in settings)
   ── Audio: music + ambience + voice ....... ~1.0 GB
      (3 radio stations ~3 h original/CC0 music, ambient beds,
       TTS voice lines as WAV/OGG at 48 kHz)
   ── Misc: videos-as-image-cards, fonts .... ~0.5 GB
RULES: no zero-byte padding, no duplicated assets to inflate size, no random
junk files. If genuine content lands at e.g. 7.8 GB, SHIP 7.8 GB and state the
honest number in the report. Quality > target. (The HD pack is where bulk
honestly lives — generate proper 4K procedural materials, not noise.)

# SECTION 10 — TECHNICAL CONSTITUTION

10.1 ENGINE: **Godot 4.3+ (GDScript primary, C# optional)** — chosen because it
     exports Windows .exe cleanly, is scriptable headlessly, and runs light.
     (If the environment cannot run Godot export templates: FALLBACK LADDER in
     Section 17.3. Do NOT silently switch to a web-only deliverable — the .exe
     is a contract.)
10.2 RENDERING PATH: Vulkan Forward+ where available, Compatibility/GL
     fallback for Low preset. Baked lightmaps for interiors + key exteriors
     where affordable; DirectionalLight3D + SSAO only on High/Ultra.
10.3 ARCHITECTURE (folders of the source repo):

   /game                  Godot project root (project.godot)
     /scenes              every .tscn (player, enemies, vehicles, levels, UI)
     /scripts
       /core              autoload singletons: SaveManager, AudioManager,
                          InputMapper, SettingsManager, EventBus, TimeOfDay
       /player            movement, combat, interaction
       /ai                enemy FSM/BT, traffic, pedestrians, wildlife
       /vehicles          handling, damage, radio
       /missions          objective system, triggers, checkpoints
       /ui                HUD, menus, map screen
     /assets
       /models            glTF/GLB (self-made + Section 13 packs)
       /textures          PNG/WEBP + HD pack under /textures_hd
       /audio             /sfx /music /voice
       /fonts
     /data                JSON/balancing: weapons.json, economy.json,
                          missions.json, skills.json, spawn_tables.json
     /tools               build scripts, import scripts, test harness
     /tests               GUT or scripted smoke tests (headless)
   /build                 export output (gitignored)
   /dist                  final zip deliverable (gitignored)

10.4 DATA-DRIVEN RULE: balance numbers live in /data JSON — never hardcode
     weapon damage or shop prices in scripts.
10.5 SAVE FORMAT: JSON, versioned {"schema": 1, ...}, forward-migratable.
10.6 DETERMINISM-DIMM: fixed physics tick 60 Hz; gameplay logic on tick,
     rendering interpolated.

# SECTION 11 — CODE STANDARDS

- Typed GDScript; one responsibility per script; max ~300 lines/file else split.
- All connections via EventBus signals where decoupling helps testability.
- No busy-work nodes: pooling for bullets, decals, corpses (cap 16 corpses).
- Comment the tricky physics (vehicle tuning, wingsuit lift) with the WHY.
- Every system ships with its smoke test (Section 18.0).

# SECTION 12 — INPUT

Keyboard/mouse + gamepad (XInput-style). Rebindable. Sensitivity + acceleration
sliders. Auto-detect device swap with glyph swap (keyboard vs Xbox-style).

# SECTION 13 — ASSET STRATEGY (LICENSE-SAFE SOURCES)

You may pull, bundle, and credit:
  - **Kenney** (kenney.nl) — CC0: UI packs, vehicles, characters ("Blocky"
    style packs), weapons, audio packs. THE backbone.
  - **Quaternius** (quaternius.com) — CC0 low-poly nature, city, characters,
    universal animation library.
  - **KayKit** (kaylousberg.itch.io) — CC-BY character/prop packs (adventure,
    skeletons, pirate-ish props recolorable away from any IP).
  - **Mixamo** — auto-rig + animations for the humanoid (license: usable in
    games; no redistribution of the raw service, embed in project fine).
  - **Sonniss GDC Audio Bundles** (sonniss.com/gameaudiogdc) — royalty-free
    AAA SFX packs (GBs of honest, legal content — great for the 10 GB goal).
  - **Free Music Archive / Kevin MacLeod (CC-BY) / Godot community SFX** —
    radio tracks (KEEP A LICENSES.md FILE LISTING EVERY ASSET'S ORIGIN).
  - Procedural: terrain (FastNoiseLite), city layout (grammar-based block
    generator), 4K materials (self-authored shader graphs), decals, rocks.
PLACEHOLDER POLICY: if a pack is unreachable in the sandbox, build a programmatic
stand-in (capsule crew, box car with rounded shader) and list it in the report
as PLACEHOLDER — never ship silently broken visuals.

# SECTION 14 — AUDIO SPEC

- MUSIC: 3 radio stations: "COSTERA 98.5" (upbeat latin-adjacent instrumental
  CC-BY), "MERIDIAN NIGHT" (synth/lofi), "LA VOZ DEL PUEBLO" (talk radio:
  TTS-voiced news bulletins that react to your mission progress + parody ads).
  ~30–45 licensed/CC tracks total across stations. Vehicle radio + safehouse
  cassette + pause-menu duck.
- SFX: footsteps by material (grass/metal/wood/water), weapon set (fire, reload,
  empty, holster), impacts, doors, punches, grunts, wildlife loops, rain/thunder.
- VOICE: TTS-generated (system `espeak-ng`/`piper`/edge-tts where available) for
  mission briefings, Sereno monologues, radio DJ. WAV/OGG 48 kHz, mixed under
  ducking. Subtitles always available (accessibility + covers missing voice).

# SECTION 15 — PERFORMANCE CONTRACT (PRIORITY P4)

15.1 LAUNCH TARGETS ("perfect on low-end"): 60 FPS @ 1280x720, Low preset, on
     integrated graphics (Intel UHD 620-class / AMD Vega 8). Your i7 CPU is
     plenty; budgets below are GPU-sandbagged accordingly.
15.2 BUDGET TABLE (per rendered frame, open world):
     draw calls < 900 (Low) / < 1500 (High) ·
     visible triangles < 1.2 M (Low) / < 3 M (High) ·
     shadow casters: directional cascade only (Low: 1 cascade, 40 m) ·
     texture memory < 1 GB (Low, HD pack OFF) / < 3.5 GB (Ultra + HD) ·
     particles: pooled, hard caps (rain 2 k, impacts 64) ·
     AI active agents: 24 (Low) / 48 (High) — simulate sleep beyond 150 m.
15.3 QUALITY PRESETS (Low/Medium/High/Ultra) each toggling: shadows, SSAO,
     AA (FXAA→TAA), draw distance (60/100/160/250 m), foliage density,
     traffic/ped counts, HD textures. Auto-detect first-run recommend Low on
     integrated GPUs.
15.4 STABILITY: no leak over 30 min idle (pool everything), no null-crash on
     save/load, no fall-through-world on all spawn points (validation test).

zipline, fall dmg). Third graybox parkour course. ✅ DoD: course under 30 s
feels FUN; fps stable; input rebind works.

damage zones, enemy grunts with PATROL→COMBAT, health pickups, death/respawn.
✅� DoD: 30-enemy arena playable; kill loop satisfying; no NaN damage bugs.

**M3 — ISLAND VERTICAL SLICE**: 1 km² island block: beach, jungle, 1 outpost
full stealth-capable, 1 mast, wildlife 2 species, day/night. ✅ DoD: you can
scout, plan, and capture the outpost 3 valid ways (stealth/loud/mixed) —
THIS MILESTONE IS THE GAME'S QUALITY BAR.

**M4 — CITY VERTICAL SLICE**: 8 city blocks + harbor stub, 3 drivable vehicles
with arcade handling + radio stub, traffic 15 cars, peds 30, heat system 0→5
with cruisers, 1 odd-job. ✅ DoD: steal car → evade 3 stars → buy hot dog at
stand with stolen cash. THAT loop must grin.

**M5 — SYSTEMS & PROGRESSION**: economy, shops, safehouse, skills 6.8,
crafting, wanted persistence, island alert level, map screen + fog, fast
travel. ✅ DoD: 45 min play session with real meta progression.

**M6 — STORY & CONTENT BUILD**: all 11 missions (Section 7), both full maps
(4.1/4.2 content budgets), side content suites, cutscene-lite + TTS voice,
2 endings. ✅ DoD: complete playthrough 8–12 h.

**M7 — LIFE & POLISH**: full audio pass (Section 14), wildlife/flocks, panic
systems, camera FX, controller glyphs, UI/UX sweep, subtitles, difficulty
modes, achievements-lite in Extras. ✅ DoD: nothing feels placeholder.

**M8 — PERFORMANCE, HD PACK & SHIP**: profiling pass vs 15.2, quality presets
tuned, HD 4K material pack generated (Section 9), crash-proofing (Section 18),
final QA suite green, packaging 17.1–17.2, README_PLAYER.md. ✅ DoD: Section 18
acceptance = 100% green. SHIP.

# SECTION 17 — BUILD & DELIVERY CONTRACT (THE .EXE PROMISE)

17.1 BUILD PIPELINE (`/tools/build.sh` and `build.ps1`):
     - headless Godot import → export "Windows Desktop" preset (release).
     - outputs `build/win/DividedHorizon.exe` + `DividedHorizon.pck`
       (embed option allowed: single .exe if template permits).
     - zips `dist/DividedHorizon-win64.zip`.
17.2 EXACT DELIVERABLE TREE (the files you hand over — "amongst the files it
     will create"):

   DividedHorizon-win64/
     DividedHorizon.exe            ← THE game (double-click to play)
     DividedHorizon.pck            ← game data (omit if embedded)
     /docs/README_PLAYER.md        ← how to run, controls, settings
     /docs/LICENSES.md             ← every asset license (Section 13)
     /docs/CHANGELOG.md
     /textures_hd/                 ← optional HD pack (Section 9)
     /tools/uninstall_notes.txt    ← it's portable: delete folder to remove

   REQUIREMENTS to state in README_PLAYER.md: Windows 10/11 x64, any DX11/Vulkan
   GPU (integrated OK), ~10 GB disk, 8 GB RAM. "If it doesn't boot: run with
   --safe-mode flag to force Compatibility renderer."

17.3 FALLBACK LADDER (only if Windows export templates cannot run in the
     build environment — still deliver an .exe by any rung):
     (a) download official Godot export templates matching engine version →
     (b) cross-compile a raylib/SDL native mini-build of M4 slice as
         `DividedHorizon_Slice.exe` via MinGW-w64 AND continue engine build
         with `build.ps1` for the user's PC →
     (c) Electron/Tauri wrap of an HTML5 export as last resort `.exe`.
     NEVER deliver a folder with no .exe. Report which rung you used.

# SECTION 18 — ACCEPTANCE SUITE ("PERFECT" = THIS, 100% GREEN)

18.0 SMOKE TESTS (headless, run every milestone): boot to menu; new game loads;
     each system's unit test (save/load round-trip, damage math, heat transitions,
     vehicle enter/exit, mission objective chain).
18.1 BOOT: clean double-click boot < 20 s to menu on min-spec. --safe-mode works.
18.2 STABILITY: 60-min session, no crash, no leak (>10% RSS growth = fail),
     alt-tab safe, windowed/fullscreen toggle safe, quit saves cleanly.
18.3 PERFORMANCE: 60 FPS avg @ 720p Low on integrated-class GPU (or document
     measured numbers if the build machine's GPU is the only test rig).
18.4 COMBAT: all 8 weapons fire/reload/empty states; takedowns; throwables;
     death/respawn/checkpoint integrity (no lost progress).
18.5 STEALTH: full loop (tag → takedown → hide) works on outpost AND city
     mission; detection meter UI truthful.
18.6 VEHICLES: all classes drive/damage/explode; radio changes stations;
     traffic survives 15-min soak; 5-star evasion reachable and escapable.
18.7 ECONOMY/PROGRESSION: buy/sell; skills unlock and apply; save → quit →
     load restores position, inventory, world state.
18.8 CONTENT: 11/11 missions completable start→end on Normal; both endings;
     all 12 outposts capturable; all 3 strongholds; at least 12 side missions.
18.9 UX: rebind persists; gamepad plays start-to-finish; subtitles on; FOV
     slider works; quality presets switch live without restart-crash.
18.10 LEGAL: LICENSES.md complete; zero third-party IP strings (grep your own
      repo for "GTA|FarCry|Rockstar|Ubisoft|Marvel|Spider" — must only hit
      this document's history or be absent).
18.11 DELIVERY: `dist/DividedHorizon-win64.zip` extracts → .exe launches on a
      clean Windows profile (no Godot install needed). Honest size reported.

# SECTION 19 — FAILURE PROTOCOL (WHEN YOU HIT A WALL)

If a system can't be completed in the environment (no export templates, no GPU
for profiling, pack download blocked):
  1. DO NOT fake it (no stub named like the real thing without saying so).
  2. Implement the DOWNGRADED but working version (box car > missing car).
  3. Log it in the milestone report: BLOCKED — reason — workaround used —
     what the user should do (e.g., run `build.ps1` on their PC).
  4. Keep the game runnable (P5). A milestone is late or downsized, never broken.

# SECTION 20 — WORKING PROTOCOL (HOW YOU OPERATE)

STEP 1  Reply FIRST with: architecture summary, risk list, and your M0 plan.
        Wait? NO — assume approval and BUILD M0 immediately after the plan.
STEP 2  Work milestone-by-milestone. After each: run smoke tests, run the game
        headlessly (and interactively if a preview exists), then WRITE
        `/docs/reports/MX_report.md` (done / stubs / deviations / fps notes).
STEP 3  Commit per milestone on branch `arena/01a0d528-game` with message
        `M#: title`. Never leave the tree broken.
STEP 4  On M8: run full Section 18 suite, produce the zip, and reply with:
        FINAL REPORT — honest scoreboard vs this spec, the deliverable tree,
        measured FPS table, known gaps (there WILL be some — name them), and
        how to build from source if the prebuilt fails.

COMMANDS YOU WILL USE CONSTANTLY:
  godot --headless --import . && godot --headless --export-release "Windows Desktop" build/win/DividedHorizon.exe
  godot --headless -s tests/run_smoke.gd
  ./tools/build.sh   # one-shot: import → test → export → zip

# SECTION 21 — THE FIRST MESSAGE YOU SEND BACK TO ME

Kick off with exactly these headings:
  ▸ PLAN — 10 lines max summarizing your approach
  ▸ STACK — engine/version confirmed, export strategy confirmed
  ▸ RISKS — top 3, each with mitigation
  ▸ M0 — begin now, show the tree you created and the first build log

#  PART II — THE GREATNESS DOCTRINE
#  ("...and the whole world will love it" — engineered, not wished for)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# SECTION 22 — AMBITION TIERS & TRIAGE (the discipline that makes greatness possible)

Every Part II feature is tagged:
[SHIP] — must exist at launch. Hard requirement.
[LOVE] — the features players beg their friends to try. Implement after the
  corresponding [SHIP] system is green. Target: ALL of them.
[DREAM] — stretch goals of legend. Build only when [LOVE] is green and tests
  pass. If cut, list honestly in the final report as "next patch".
TRIAGE LAW: Section 18 acceptance is sacred — never trade stability, perf,
  or the .exe for a [DREAM]. A smaller finished game beats a bigger broken one.
GREATNESS LAW: what you ship must be LOVED, not checked off. Every feature
  below ships with juice (Section 32 sound language + game-feel polish) or it
  does not ship at all.

# SECTION 23 — THE SOUL: WRITING & TONE BIBLE [SHIP]

The world loves games with heart before graphics. Writing rules:
23.1 TONE: adventure with warmth and dry humor. Banter, not quips-at-gunpoint.
  Violence has weight and consequence, never glee. The island is beautiful AND
  exploited; the city is dazzling AND cruel. Show both honestly.
23.2 HUMOR: character-driven (Poncho stealing a fish; Hands' terrible
  metaphors; El Sereno offering you tea mid-siege). NEVER punch down. No
  ethnic, gender, disability, or "funny accent" jokes. The setting is a love
  letter to tropical Latin-American/Caribbean rhythms — treat it with respect,
  not costume (checklist in 34.3). No joke that only works in English may sit
  on the critical path (translation-proof humor).
23.3 HEART: Ren's arc is "the man who walks away learns to stand with others."
  Quiet moments are spec'd, not accidental: brother voicemails at campfires,
  Marisol teaching you dominoes, Poncho asleep on your boots during menu idle.
23.4 EL SERENO: one of the great QUIET villains. He never once screeches.
  He offers real philosophy (community vs freedom) so players ARGUE about him
  online for years. Rule: he believes he is the hero of Isla Sombra.
23.5 CONTENT KINDNESS: menu toggle for reduced gore; chapter-head content
  warnings; skip buttons on everything. Respect the player's nervous system.
23.6 ENDINGS (7): both core endings + Stature epilogue variant (26.4) must
  each feel EARNED and discuss-worthy. No "golden ending" that belittles the
  rest. No achievement for suffering (see 29.4).

# SECTION 24 — PONCHO, THE COMPANION DOG [SHIP]
# (Universally beloved feature #1. Budget for him first.)

A caramel mutt found in Act I mission 2, stuck in a poacher trap. Free him —
he adopts you forever.
- FOLLOW AI: navmesh travel with teleport-safety (never lost >8 s; edge fades).
- COMMANDS: stay / follow / fetch / hunt (sniff-highlights loot within 20 m) /
  distract (bark pulls one enemy's state toward him).
- COMBAT: cannot die. Knocked out → limps to cover → revive with a 3 s hold,
  or auto-recovers in 30 s. Enemies shove, never shoot him.
- JOY BEHAVIORS: digs up buried caches (20 cache markers), rolls in mud
  (cosmetic dirt until he swims), plays with street dogs downtown, HOWLS with
  police sirens at 4+ stars (players will clip this forever), yawns when you
  aim too long.
- FISHING BUDDY (31): waits on the dock; celebrates every catch.
- BOND LEVELS 1–5 (pet / play / feed): bigger sniff radius, trick emote wheel
  (sit, paw, roll over, "speak" bark-on-command), collar cosmetics (Bandana
  Red, Marigold, Golden Collar @ 100% — see 38).
- VOICE: 14 bark/whine/woof/airplane-ear-whimper variants, pitch randomization.
- UI: Paw icon near HUD showing his state. Photo Mode poses (28). His collar
  jingles when he runs — predicted #1 smile trigger in playtests.

# SECTION 25 — THE FIVE BOSSES + EL SERENO [SHIP] (fights with personality)

Every boss: intro cutscene-lite (23 rules), pre-fight radio banter, arena with
a 3-phase gimmick, and mid-fight barks reacting to player style (stealth
players hear "WHERE ARE YOU?" search lines; loud players get impressed grunts).
B1 "LA SARGENTO" MARTA QUILES — sugar-mill fortress in the rain. Drill-caller
   who orders shield-wall formations; gimmick: break her cadence by shooting
   the call-drums — silence is the damage phase.
B2 "EL RELOJ" — clocktower bomb-maker. Four bomb-cycles to close blast doors
   on him; gimmick: rotating clock-hand platforms — time itself is the hazard.
   The planned most-clipped fight of the game.
B3 "DOÑA MAREA" — harbor queen. Boat-and-docks pursuit: she rams with an
   armored gunship; gimmick: harpoon fuel lines between passes, then fight
   her on the burning pier. Sunset lighting mandatory.
B4 "EL FRAILE" — silent giant. Bell-tower duel; melee-parry gimmick. Church
   bells audio-telegraph his swings (accessibility by design — 33).
B5 "EL LIMPIADOR" (city finale) — commander of Task Force Limpio. Armored
   downtown assault: SWAT vans reposition as cover; EMP the block to expose him.
FINALE — EL SERENO: lighthouse, monsoon. (i) Calm speech + waves of cultists
   while YOUR liberated garrisons (26.1) hold the flanks — the world you saved
   fights beside you; (ii) he fights — cane blade, precise, polite; (iii) the
   choice (7). Adaptive score (32) drops to near-silence during his speeches.
   Goosebumps are a spec requirement.

# SECTION 26 — THE EMERGENCE ENGINE (a world that gossips about you)

26.1 FACTION WAR SIM: every region has CONTROL % across LA COSECHA / CARTEL
  DEL FARO / MERIDIAN PD / THE PEOPLE. Your actions shift it (loud outpost
  −15% cult; stealth liberation +5% people; police raids bleed cartels). At
  thresholds: guard types flip, shop prices shift (−20% when THE PEOPLE hold),
  street graffiti changes, headlines rewrite (27), and radio editorials turn.
26.2 LOS COBRADORES (nemesis-lite bounty squad) [LOVE]: push Infamy high and
  4 named hunters come for YOU in free roam: "RASTRA" (tracker with a hound —
  Poncho growls before you see her), "VIDENTE" (glass-cannon rooftop sniper),
  "EL PULPO" (netgun brute + 2 goons), "LUCKY" (con-artist who flags you down
  pretending to be a tourist). They remember: wounded hunters return with
  scars and new lines; each falls for good after 3 encounters. Clear the squad
  → unique trinket + Herald obituaries.
26.3 DYNAMIC FREE-ROAM EVENTS [LOVE]: 24 templates, district-weighted,
  cooldown-managed: bank alarm you can join or foil, cartel convoy ambush/
  hijack, street-race signups, a stranded tourist with a suspicious sob story,
  pickpocket chases, a beach rave at night (music swells, dance emote
  encouraged), sudden monsoon stampedes. The island and city are stages, not
  spreadsheets.
26.4 STATURE (Hero ↔ Renegade) [SHIP]: a hidden slider shifted by mercy,
  rescues, thefts, and massacres. It changes crowd greetings (cheer vs
  scatter), radio editorials, prices, which NPCs live to see the finale, and
  the EPILOGUE VARIANT (a third closing cut). Poncho is proud of you either
  way. Never preach — observe.

# SECTION 27 — THE MERIDIAN HERALD + REACTIVE MEDIA [LOVE]

- Procedural NEWSPAPER front pages after every major action: templated
  headlines populated with your real stats ("MASKED FIGURE SINKS 3 BOATS —
  witnesses describe 'unnecessarily cool'"). Front pages stack at your
  safehouse as a SCRAPBOOK — flip through your own legend.
- Kiosks show today's headline in-world; "LA VOZ DEL PUEBLO" reads them on
  air with TTS editorializing (awe or horror per Stature, 26.4).
- WANTED BOARD: your evolving police-sketch portrait (procedural collage of
  facial parts — gloriously bad composite energy), bounty rising with Infamy.
  Screenshot bait; exports watermark-free via share cards (28).

# SECTION 28 — PHOTO MODE & SHARE CULTURE [SHIP]

Full photo mode: pause, free camera, FOV 15–120, DOF, exposure, 8 filters
(incl. "Cinema", "Comic", "Golden Hour", "Blueprint"), hide HUD/characters,
time-of-day scrubber at 1-min precision, 5 player poses + 3 Poncho poses [LOVE].
SHARE CARDS [LOVE]: one-click PNG poster export — screenshot + auto-caption
  pulled from current mission/state + logo + optional player tag. Folder-ready
  for socials. README prints the community prompt: #DividedHorizon.
RULE: photo mode pauses AI and simulation (perf-neutral — respects 15 budgets).

# SECTION 29 — REPLAYABILITY MACHINES

29.1 NEW GAME+ [SHIP]: carry gear + skills; world re-tunes (elite patrols,
  merged search parties, smarter flanks); side-contract locations remix;
  and Sereno KNOWS: "You've done this before, haven't you?" — one fourth-wall
  line, used exactly once, tastefully.
29.2 MUTATORS & CHEATS [LOVE], unlocked by feats: BIG HEAD MODE, LOW GRAVITY,
  PAINTBALL (recolor impacts), MIRROR WORLD, TURBO NIGHT (2× clock, neon on),
  INFINITE AMMO, PONCHO GIANT (comedy), and PHOTOREAL MODE ("it's just lens
  dirt and bloom" — an honest joke about our stylized limits; players will
  love the honesty). Combine up to 3 mutators; save is flagged (NG+ off
  while active — clearly shown, no traps).
29.3 CHAOS DIAL [LOVE]: free-roam "Heatseed" slider letting players ask for
  quiet hikes or all-out war; the Emergence Engine answers.
29.4 100% REWARDS: Golden Poncho collar, Making-Of Museum (38), true credits,
  a final quiet scene at the lighthouse at sunrise. NO collectible-torture
  (nothing spawns at 900 of anything).

# SECTION 30 — ENDLESS MERCENARY CONTRACTS + SPEEDRUN KIT [LOVE]

- CONTRACT BOARD: procedural jobs from 12 archetypes (escort, takedown-only,
  convoy, time trial, bounty, stealth-theft, horde holdout, photo-journalism,
  animal rescue, demolition, street race, mystery delivery) × both maps × 6
  difficulty mutators. Seeded PRNG.
- CONTRACT OF THE DAY: seed derived from the local date — everyone worldwide
  gets the same daily contract. Shared bragging rights, zero servers.
- SPEEDRUN KIT: run timer + autosplits per mission, local best-times board,
  IL practice mode (restart any mission instantly), race ghost data [DREAM:
  full player-ghost replays], deterministic seeds.

# SECTION 31 — MINIGAMES & SIDEWAYS JOY [LOVE]

- FISHING: 8 species, relaxed untimed mode + marina tourneys, fish journal
  with pun names (El Pez Gordo), Poncho reactions (24).
- DOMINOES: block-dominoes vs NPCs at Alto Barro tables, with banter lines.
- ARCADE CABINETS ×3 in city bars — full little games with local highscores:
  SERPIENTE (snake-likes), LADRILLOS (breakout-likes), CHISMOSO (dodger).
- SHOOTING GALLERIES at the island festival (precision leaderboards).
- JUKEBOX at every safehouse: choose station or exact track (32).
- Night-market DICE bluff [DREAM].
LAW: every minigame is small, polished, and exits in one button.

# SECTION 32 — DYNAMIC MUSIC & AUDIO SOUL [SHIP core]

- ADAPTIVE SCORE: vertical layers crossfade on intensity (ambient →
  exploration → stealth-pulse → combat-brass → triumph-sting), 1-bar grid.
  Island: marimba/strings/surf-guitar. City: synth-bass/jazz-noir/latin
  percussion. Finale: choir swell from licensed/synthesized sources only.
- RADIO: the 3 stations of Section 14 + DJ chatter reacting to live world
  state (26/27) ("...and whoever's been out at the sugar mill? Dios mío.").
- SPATIAL AUDIO: reverb zones (caves, cathedrals, open sea), occlusion, rain
  on roofs vs open water, indoor low-pass on sirens and music.
- UI SOUND LANGUAGE: every action crisp and readable; Poncho's collar jingle
  (24); stingers for outposts liberated that the whole map can "hear".

# SECTION 33 — ACCESSIBILITY FOR EVERY HUMAN [SHIP core]
# ("the whole world will love it" starts with the players the industry forgets)

CORE [SHIP]:
  • Full input remap + hold/toggle EVERYWHERE (sprint, aim, crouch, interact).
  • Subtitles: size, colors, background opacity, speaker colors, max-cps.
  • Colorblind modes (deuter/prot/trit + custom channel mixer) + custom
    reticle shape/color.
  • Photosensitivity mode: kills screen shake, strobes, bloom spikes,
    chromatic FX (18.9b: verify by grepping your own shake/flash callsites).
  • FOV 70–110; UI scale 80–150%; safe-area calibration; gamma pattern test.
  • Difficulty presets + INDEPENDENT sliders (enemy damage / aim / detection
    speed / puzzle timers). Auto-win QTE option. Global slow-motion 50–100%.
  • Dyslexia-friendly font (OpenDyslexic-class). Aim assist + magnetism.
  • One-handed keyboard preset (WASD+mouse-only reachable core loop).
LOVE: TTS UI NARRATION (menus + HUD spoken); low-vision audio-cue suite
  (objective sonar pings, enemy-alert heartbeats, loot shimmer, Poncho
  proximity jingle); CO-PILOT ASSIST (two controllers, one player — helper
  drives while you aim); high-contrast enemy outline shader.
DREAM: full blind-playable traversal audio lattice (3D pings for ledges,
  vaults, interactables) + shipped self-critical accessibility report.
GAMER LAW: EVERY Part II feature must work with photosensitivity mode, UI
  scale, and remapping. No exceptions. No excuses.

# SECTION 34 — LOCALIZATION & CULTURALIZATION (loving "the whole world" = speaking to it)

34.1 i18n PIPELINE [SHIP]: every string in gettext-style tables. Ship 12–14
  languages: EN, ES (LATAM flavor), PT-BR, FR, DE, IT, PL, TR, ZH-Hans, JA,
  KO, HI, AR (full RTL layout pass — not a bolt-on). Machine-translated first
  pass is acceptable IF flagged in reports; native-speaker review is [LOVE].
34.2 VOICE: English TTS voice lines; other languages receive subtitles plus
  TTS dubbing where quality permits [LOVE]. Nothing critical depends on
  audio-only information (33).
34.3 CULTURAL RESPECT CHECKLIST (run before content lock — sign it off):
  no accent-as-comedy; names reviewed for real-world harm; religion handled
  with gravity (La Cosecha is a corruption OF faith — faithful civilians and
  a decent village priest exist in the world too); music credited and
  licensed; if a native reviewer would wince, rewrite it.
34.4 PRONUNCIATION & DUB GUIDE for TTS lexicon and future human dubs
  (El Sereno = "el seh-REH-no"), shipped in /docs.

# SECTION 35 — KINDNESS AS A FEATURE (how a game earns love)

- DRM-FREE. Offline-complete. No accounts, no telemetry, no ads, no store,
  no FOMO timers, no engagement dark patterns. Ever.
- SKIPPABLE everything. PAUSABLE everything (incl. cutscenes). Save anywhere
  out of combat (auto-rollback if you save mid-fall — physics-tested).
- PRICE ETHIC: free / pay-what-you-want with a donation placeholder; source
  repo stays public; every asset credited (38).
- STABILITY IS RESPECT: the crash-free promise (18.2) is a kindness feature.
- THE 11 PM TEST: every system must pass "would a tired player at 11 PM
  smile?" If no: redesign or cut it.

- SHARE CONTRACT: export a daily seed (30) as JSON for friends.

#  PART III — THE MASTERPIECE DOCTRINE
#  (Turning a great game into the one they'll still talk about in 2036)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# SECTION 42 — THE PINNACLE PRINCIPLES (read before any pixel)

42.1 THE FIVE MOMENTS LAW: the game must contain five spec'd setpieces (52)
  so strong that every player tells a friend about at least one. Everything
  else exists to support those five minutes.
42.2 THE POSTCARD LAW: every named vista must be composed like a postcard —
  landmark in frame, foreground silhouette, horizon rule. If a screenshot of
  it wouldn't stop a scroll, redesign the sightline.
42.3 THE SILHOUETTE LAW: characters and vehicles read at 50 m as pure
  silhouettes (no color needed). Cut nothing from shape language to save polys
  — cut texture detail instead.
42.4 THE 11 PM TEST (35) and TRIAGE LAW (22) remain supreme. A masterpiece
  is a FINISHED work with soul. Unfinished ambition is just a demo.
42.5 SEED EARLY: Sections 43, 44, and 47 are STANDARDS that apply from M0
  onward — they are not a late pass. M12 enforces the lock; it does not
  invent the quality.

# SECTION 43 — THE AESTHETIC BIBLE [SHIP core]

43.1 COLOR SCRIPTS (paint these as HUD-independent world lighting):
   ACT I — Isla Sombra: jade & gold. Warm dawn haze, saturated flora, sea
     shifting turquoise→deep ultramarine. Sunsets are the reward loop.
   ACT II — Meridian City: cyan & magenta rain-slick neon on wet asphalt,
     sodium-orange harbor, cold marble Downtown, dusk-lit marina.
   ACT III — Return: storm violet + lighthouse gold cutting through rain.
     The palette narrows to the two colors of the finale.
43.2 LIGHTING: every exterior gets a dawn/dusk "hero lighting" pass; golden
   hour is gameplay (long shadows aid stealth — AI vision cones already use
   them). Interiors: motivated sources only (a lamp lights like a lamp).
43.3 STYLE: painterly stylized (NOT photoreal — proudly, consistently).
   Materials read like brush-blocked gouache at 5 m and clean color at 50 m.
   "No brown soup": mud is ochre, concrete is warm grey, foliage is 3 greens.
43.4 SILHOUETTE & SHAPE: cult = tall angular hats and long coats; Cartel =
   slick sportswear and fast shapes; PD = boxy authority; Ren = one field
   poncho silhouette (cloth sim, 48) + shoulder holster. Poncho the dog =
   triangle ears, question-mark tail. Recognizable in shadow.
43.5 UI ART: tropical print / civic-signage motif — enamel badges, stenciled
   crate letters, hand-tourist-map cartography (50). The map screen is a
   gallery piece: districts illustrated like mural postcards.
43.6 PHOTO MODE FILTERS inherit the color script ("Golden Hour", "Monsoon",
   "Cinema") so player screenshots share our palette by default.

# SECTION 44 — MOTION & GAME-FEEL [SHIP core] (juice is not optional)

44.1 ANIMATION LAW: 12 principles applied to stylized rigs — anticipation on
   swings, follow-through on poncho cloth and Poncho's tail, settle > snap.
   Animation states must blend; pops are bugs unless they're COMEDY pops.
44.2 IMPACT TABLE (tuned, then verified per weapon): hit-stop 40–80 ms,
   camera punch 2–6°, damage flash <3 frames (photosensitivity-safe alt),
   controller rumble L/R patterns per weapon class. A shotgun must FEEL like
   a promise kept.
44.3 THE GAZE RULE: characters' eyes LOOK at what matters (IK gaze targets:
   who's speaking, the threat, the horizon). Stylized + alive beats realistic
   + vacant. Poncho tracks Ren's hand when it has food. Spec that line.
44.4 FOOT IK on slopes/stairs; hand IK on vehicle wheels and railings; no
   moon-walks, no hover-shoes outside of mutators.
44.5 CAMERA: one tuned follow for movement, one for ADS, one for vehicles
   (separate FOV + shake stacks, all respecting 33 photosensitivity mode).
   Dodge-roll gets a speedline accent. Boss entrances own the camera.
44.6 COMEDY PHYSICS ALLOWLIST (exact budget): wet-floor slips, market-orange
   explosions, rubber-duck buoyancy (54), Poncho-vs-hose battle. Comedy is
   choreographed, never random-jank.

# SECTION 45 — WATER, WIND & WORLD TECH [SHIP]

45.1 WATER SYSTEM (the island's face card): Gerstner wave stacks with LOD,
   shoreline foam matching beach slope, cheap refraction-lite, boat wakes,
   rain ripple rings, waterfall mist volumes. Water must read beautiful at
   dawn (43.2) — this is a Five-Moments dependency (52 #1).
45.2 THE UNDERWORLD [LOVE]: four dive zones with their own palette & music
   layer: (a) CORAL GARDEN (fish schools = boids), (b) THE GALLEON "Santa
   Promesa" (lore wreck, air-pocket caves, Tobias cache), (c) BLUE HOLE
   (cave descent — the legend, 54), (d) SUBMERGED CULT VAULT (Sereno's
   drowned reliquary — story). Breath meter, dive knife, underwater takedowns
   (muted, eerie). Treasure maps surface to 49's walls.
45.3 WIND SYSTEM: vertex-wind on grass/palms/coat cloth, gust events that
   push loose debris and drown dialogue with audio ducking (players will
   lean in — trust it). Storm gusts in Act III gameplay-affect (missile
   arcs bow, ziplines sway).
45.4 PERFORMANCE: water and wind obey Section 15 budgets — wave LOD by
   camera height (boat view = full, vista = normal, Low preset = 2 waves).
   Never drop frames during the Five Moments (52). Ever.

# SECTION 46 — ECOLOGY & SCHEDULES [LOVE] (the sim layer of love)

46.1 NAMED-NPC SCHEDULES: every named NPC has a day (Marisol's workshop
   8:00–20:00, dominoes at dusk; Hands' fence vanishes at night; the priest
   of the good chapel rings noon). Vendors close. Bars fill. Guards rotate.
   The world has BEDTIMES — players notice and adore it.
46.2 ECOLOGY LITE: grazer herds → island predators → scavengers; overhunting
   thins species (hunting stays, judgment stays silent, recovery timer real).
   Bird flocks flee gunfire; Poncho flushes rabbits (comedy + hunting aid).
46.3 DOG PACKS: city strays respect Poncho (bond-dependent growls or play).
   RASTRA's hound (26.2) is the one dog he cannot charm — spec that rivalry.
46.4 WEATHER REACTIVITY: rain fills street puddles (splash quads), crowds
   thin to awnings, island trails get mud-slide shortcuts [DREAM], and the
   fishing table rerolls (fish bite better in rain — folklore made code).

# SECTION 47 — ENVIRONMENTAL STORYTELLING LAW [SHIP core]

47.1 THE 3-READ RULE: every one of the 12 outposts + 3 strongholds + key
   interiors must tell its micro-story three ways: (1) DISTANCE read —
   silhouette + banner + what's burning; (2) APPROACH read — patrols, props,
   sound (a radio arguing, a generator failing); (3) INTERIOR read — the
   human evidence (letters, medicine bottles, two chairs and one empty).
   40 micro-stories live as data in /data as envstories.json.
47.2 SERMON TAPES (12, collectible): El Sereno's philosophy in his own calm
   voice — the kind players transcribe and debate. Never strawman him (23.4).
47.3 TOBIAS'S TAPES (8): the brother's voicemail arc — funny, then worried,
   then brave. Played whole at 100% (38). The heart of the game may be a
   voice on a radio.
47.4 ALTO BARRO GRAFFITI: the slums tell their own story in murals —
   including foreshadowing and one mural that's the community puzzle (54).

# SECTION 48 — PHYSICS TOYS & DESTRUCTION [LOVE]

- BREAKABLES BUDGET: crates, glass, fences, market stalls (oranges must
  scatter — mandated 44.6), arcade cabinets smoke when shot (the ones in 31
  go dark, then a tech appears — small mercy moment).
- CHAIN REACTIONS: fuel drums, gas stoves, ammo caches — telegraphed, fair,
  spectacular. Explosions have falloff + heat-haze + a duck-worthy quack if
  a rubber duck (54) is in radius.
- REN'S FIELD PONCHO: real cloth sim (the game's namesake — do not skip),
  wind-reactive (45.3), wet state darkening. Dyeable endgame.
- RAGDOLL + authored get-up blends; corpses pooled (11.3); ragdoll ORIGAMI
  mutator [DREAM]: everyone folds like paper on death (comedy mode).
- ZIPLINE/Rope: verlet lines with sway; hand-over-hand rider animation set.

# SECTION 49 — SAFEHOUSE EVOLUTION [LOVE] (watch yourself grow)

THREE safehouses that physically upgrade with your legend:
  ISLAND CABIN → stocked workbench (crafting 6.8), drying racks, map table
  with pins of captured outposts, Poncho's bed (straw → cushion → throne).
  MERIDIAN LOFT above the print shop (the Herald prints downstairs — your
  scrapbook (27) grows in real time), garage for owned vehicles (3 cars +
  bike display slots).
  LIGHTHOUSE KEEPER'S FLAT (finale hub, unlocked Act III).
TROPHY WALL: no animal heads — mementos with meaning: El Reloj's cracked
  watch, Doña Marea's brass spyglass, Sereno's teacup (if you know, you
  know). Boss mementos + feat plaques (53) + treasure maps (45.2) + the
  duck shelf (54). Visitors [DREAM: Steam screenshots of this wall become
  the fandom].

# SECTION 50 — UI AS LOVE LANGUAGE [SHIP core]

50.1 HUD: minimal core (health arc, ammo, paw icon (24), contextual prompt)
  + optional full minimap/compass. Diegetic-lite mode folds heat stars into
  radio static intensity and stealth eye into audio ducking.
50.2 THE MAP: hand-illustrated tourist map (43.5) with fog lift, your pins,
  contract board (30), and postcard viewfinder previews of every vista.
50.3 MICRO-TRANSITIONS: menus slide like enamel signs; confirm stamps like a
  customs desk (Mercado motif); feats unlock with a wax-stamp animation.
50.4 SAFETY: destructive confirms name the cost ("Sell rifle 'Culebra'?"),
  autosave slots rolling (37.4), undo-safe shop (buyback window 60 s).
50.5 GLYPHS & FONTS: keyboard + Xbox-style + generic gamepad sets; dyslexic
  font (33); every number tabular-aligned; the map's compass rose secretly
  includes Poncho's silhouette (testers will find it).

# SECTION 51 — SOUNDTRACK & LEITMOTIF [SHIP core]

51.1 MINIMUM SCORE: 40+ tracks / 60+ minutes. Three signature themes:
   REN'S THEME: sea-shanty guitar fragment that rebuilds into an anthem by
     the finale.
   SERENO'S THEME: a hymn that slowly corrupts (same melody, inverted
     harmonies each act). It must play once unaccompanied — goosebumps law.
   PONCHO'S MOTIF: three-note collar jingle. MUST work as a phone ringtone,
     menu tick, and saving-grace sting when he revives (24). Spec line:
     "the game's soul is three notes."
51.2 ADAPTIVE RULES (32 extended): motifs crossfade by character presence;
  boss phases mutate their leitmotif; radio occasionally plays a cheap cover
  of Ren's Theme as a sea shanty (world-aware music — players lose it).
51.3 OST PLAYER in Extras with liner notes for each track (who plays what,
  why the hymn corrupts). Share-card export for tracks (28).

# SECTION 52 — THE FIVE MOMENTS (spec'd setpieces — 42.1) [SHIP]

MOMENT 1 — FIRST MAST SUNRISE: climb before dawn (mission 2). Summit at
  06:00 in-game: fog burns off island-wide in one continuous reveal, Ren's
  Theme's first full statement, map fogs unfurl below. Perf guard: no frame
  drops; pre-cached LOD swap ONLY for this camera path.
MOMENT 2 — TEA WITH SERENO: mission 4. A veranda, two chairs, no weapon
  drawn for 4 minutes. Pure writing + gaze animation (44.3) + the hymn
  (51.1) humming under dialogue. Players will quote this scene.
MOMENT 3 — THE CITY APOLOGY: mission 9's broadcast. You hijack every TV
  and radio in Meridian: crowds stop, cars idle, three stations go silent
  then speak together (32 radio system choreography), and the Herald's next
  front page is written by truth (27). Stature (26.4) colors the speech.
MOMENT 4 — THE MONSOON TRADE: mission 10. The storm of the century while
  you cross the island for the trade; wind (45.3) gameplay, lightning-synced
  stealth windows, Poncho refuses a puddle (comic relief timing = art).
MOMENT 5 — LIGHTHOUSE ENDING: the choice (7), Sereno's last line, silence,
  then Poncho's jingle as the only sound. Two versions + epilogue variant
  (26.4). No music under the final button press. Trust silence.
RULE: each moment ships with a written SHOT LIST + audio state table +
perf guard note in /docs/direction/five_moments.md.

# SECTION 53 — FEATS & TROPHIES [LOVE] (a poster, not a checklist)

60 FEATS, shown as a wax-stamped poster wall (50.3), never a progress bar.
Named, not numbered: "TEA WITH THE DEVIL" (Moment 2 parley), "GOOD DOG"
(bond 5), "GHOST OF ALTO BARRO" (district cleared, zero alerts), "DUEÑO
DEL MAR" (all four dive zones), "THE WORLD ACCORDING TO REN" (all Sermon
Tapes), "SUNRISE CLUB" (all masts at dawn — postcard bait), "ONE MORE
CAST" (fishing tourney), "UNNECESSARILY COOL" (Herald's most dramatic
headline about you). Secret feats unlisted ("..."). No 900-feather crimes
(29.4). Each unlock → share card (28).

# SECTION 54 — EASTER EGGS & SECRETS [LOVE] (the meme engine)

54.1 THE GREAT DUCK HUNT: 25 rubber ducks hidden across both maps (one per
  25 regions, fair placement, glint at 3 m so it's fair not cruel). Counter
  in Extras. All 25 → "CONE OF SHAME" cosmetic hat for Poncho + DUCK TUB in
  safehouse. Community will organically create the hunt map. Print the hint
  card in README: "If you find a duck, no you didn't." (Zero IP risk — ducks
  are eternal.)
54.2 ORIGINAL EGGS ONLY (LEGAL REAFFIRMED): zero references to other
  franchises — no hidden capes, mushrooms, or cake jokes belonging to
  anyone. Our eggs: room 303's silent vignette (tasteful, skippable), the
  lonely fisherman on the hidden NW islet who tells you to rest (then you
  fast-travel away and he's gone — spec the shiver), a poster of "EL SERENO:
  THE MUSICAL" in city theater (fake, beloved), Poncho growling at one
  specific harmless statue (never explained).
54.3 COMMUNITY PUZZLE [DREAM]: the Alto Barro mural (47.4) is quartered;
  four players' share-codes (36) complete it online socially — not in-game.
  The final image: a thank-you note.

# SECTION 55 — CINEMATIC CRAFT [SHIP]

55.1 CUTSCENE GRAMMAR: 180° rule, shot/reverse-shot for dialogue, no
  neck-crane orbits, motivated moves only. Stylized faces + gaze + gesture
  beats mocap-pastiche. Max 2 minutes without chapter checkpoint + skip.
55.2 TITLE SEQUENCE: cold open on black — the voicemail (7) plays whole
  over the sea's soundbed — cut to title card exactly as Ren's Theme's
  guitar enters. 40 seconds. One loading screen after. Done.
55.3 CHAPTER CARDS: hand-lettered, each with a one-line epitaph ("In which
  a dead man's dog finds a new owner" — stamp it when it lands).
55.4 THE ONE-TAKE [LOVE]: Moment 5's approach is a single unbroken long
  take (scripted cine camera, 56 tools) — the game's flex and the trailer's
  spine.

# SECTION 56 — THE MACHINIMA TOOLKIT [DREAM] (community forever-ness)

- CINE CAMERA: keyframed timeline (position/look/FOV/DOF), render-to-pngs
  or ffmpeg .mp4 (37.3b pipeline), copyable timeline JSON per Five Moment.
- REPLAY BUFFER: rolling last-30s capture (shadowplay-lite) on keyframe
  perf (cap 30 s, 720p, Low overhead).
- DEPTH/ID BUFFER export toggle for community editors' VFX fun.
- MOVIETALK: a /docs/MACHINIMA.md guide written like a film-school warm
  hand ("go make the scene we couldn't").

# SECTION 57 — THE WORLD TOUR — LAUNCH TRIO [LOVE]

(a) TRAILER: the game renders its own 90-second launch trailer via cine
  tools (56) — beat-cut to Ren's Theme, one shot from each Five Moment (52),
  Poncho's jingle as the final audio before the logo. Export .mp4 via
  bundled ffmpeg call in /tools/render_trailer.sh.
(b) PRESS KIT: /tools/gen_presskit.sh assembles /dist/presskit/: logo PNG,
  10 curated Photo Mode screenshots (43.6 palette), fact sheet (specs, size,
  price: free), benchmark table (37.1/58), and "AN HONEST LETTER" (41 voice)
  explaining stylized-vs-photoreal with pride, not apology.
(c) DEMO: "ISLA SOMBRA — 20 MINUTES" cut (37.3b) as its own zip — the
  easiest yes a stranger ever gave us.

# SECTION 58 — HARDWARE MATRIX & THE OLD-LAPTOP PLEDGE [SHIP]

58.1 TEST MATRIX (measure + publish in README):
   Intel UHD 620 (iGPU floor) · AMD Vega 8 · GTX 750 Ti · GTX 1060 ·
   RX 580 · Deck-class handheld · one "mystery potato" of the tester's
   choice. For each: AVG/1%/0.1% at 720p Low & 1080p Medium via 37.1.
58.2 THE OLD-LAPTOP PLEDGE (print in README, banner font allowed):
   "If it shipped in the last ten years with 8 GB of RAM, DIVIDED HORIZON
   runs on it. If we're wrong, file a bug and we'll fix it — free game,
   free fixes."
58.3 LOW-END TOOLKIT: --safe-mode (GL fallback), resolution scale 50–100%,
   HDD streaming budget (page-file-friendly asset chunks), "Potato HUD"
   (cheaper UI particles), first-run auto-recommend (37.2).

# SECTION 59 — THE FEEDBACK LOOP & THE LOVE PATCH [SHIP]

59.1 IN-GAME "SEND A THOUGHT": writes feedback.json locally (player chooses
  to send it — nothing leaves without consent, 35). Bug reports auto-attach
  crash-log.txt (37.4) + hardware string + settings.
59.2 THE LOVE PATCH PLEDGE: the first post-launch update is named THE LOVE
  PATCH and contains only community-requested fixes and kindness tweaks.
  Name it in the credits. Print it in HORIZON_AHEAD.md alongside the honest
  list of cut DREAM features (22) so nobody feels sold.
59.3 SELF-INFLICTED QA [SHIP]: the 30-VOW protocol — before any zip ships:
  30 full boot-to-quit runs on 3 hardware classes, one run entirely in ES,
  one with photosensitivity mode + gamepad + UI 150%, one where the tester
  plays "badly" (falls everywhere, shoots friendly signs) looking for jank.

# SECTION 60 — STREAMING & KINDNESS EXPANSION [LOVE]

- STREAM-SAFE BY DESIGN: all music/voice is original or CC — streamers may
  monetize VODs with zero fear. Print the pledge in README ("your clip of
  Poncho howling with sirens belongs to you").
- CLEAN CAPTURE MODE: HUD-less render toggle + feat toasts opt-out + mute
  licensed... (nothing to mute — it's all clean; write that with a grin).
- WELLBEING: optional session timer ("stretch prompt" every 90 min, off by
  default, never naggy), photosensitivity & subtitle defaults already ON
  where sensible (33/37.2).

# SECTION 61 — THE OPEN-SOURCE GIFT [SHIP]

- POSTMORTEM.md shipped WITH the game: honest (what worked, what didn't,
  the photoreal conversation, the milestone scars). Students will study it.
- Repo stays public with tagged source + design history. A "Built With"
  splash (Godot + every pack in 13) — skippable, credited forever (38).
- HORIZON_AHEAD.md: the respectful goodbye — what we dreamed (22 DREAM
  shelf) and what THE LOVE PATCH will carry.

FINAL REMINDER (legend edition): You now hold the complete doctrine — Parts
I–III: mechanics with the soul of island-ops and city-crime sandboxes, a
companion dog, a quiet monster over tea, a world that gossips and newspapers
that remember, kindness as a feature, accessibility for every human, twelve
languages, modding, a dog jingle that IS the soundtrack's soul, five moments
players will retell, ducks hidden in the world, a game that renders its own
trailer and writes its own postmortem — stylized, ~10 GB of honesty, 60 FPS
on a laptop from 2016, delivered as DividedHorizon.exe. "Greatest game ever
made by AI" is not a graphics setting: it is a finished game, kind to its
players, that someone finishes at 1 AM and immediately texts a friend about
a dog named Poncho. Build that. The whole world is rooting for you. ▸

#  PART IV — THE CODEX (binding world data — Parts I–III reference this)
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

# SECTION 64 — TIMELINE OF ISLA SOMBRA & MERIDIAN (the lore spine)

| Year | Event |
|------|-------|
| 1702 | Meridian founded as a free port; the harbor's first stone laid by mixed crews who refused empire. |
| 1745 | Sugar mill ("Molino Viejo") built on Isla Sombra by leaseholders; forced labor scars the ridge (remembered, never exploited for flavor). |
| 1788 | "La Santa Promesa" galleon sinks in the storm channel carrying a bell that never rang (Underworld lore, 45.2b). |
| 1810 | The Lighthouse of Isla Sombra lit; keeper's log becomes the island's first newspaper column. |
| 1856 | Meridian's Old Harbor burns once; rebuilt with the red brick that still defines it. |
| 1889 | Alto Barro hillside settled by mill families laid off after blight; the slums' mural tradition begins with protest walls. |
| 1927 | Hurricane "La Reina" levels two villages; the survivors' mutual-aid pact is the seed of island community culture. |
| 1954 | Dockside Union formed as a loading guild — legitimate for forty honest years. |
| 1968 | "Hotel Mirador" opens in Solada Beach; tourism's golden decade begins. |
| 1978 | Marisol's father opens Astillero Reyes shipyard; the family wrench becomes a heirloom (48 melee "Marisol Special"). |
| 1994 | Padre Esteban consecrates the hill chapel of Nuestra Señora del Faro — still standing, still decent (34.3). |
| 2004 | A soft-spoken young priest, TOMÁS ALCÁNTARA, arrives to assist Esteban. Preaches mutual aid. Loved. |
| 2009 | Drought + factory closures. Tomás begins feeding the whole hill out of the chapel pantry. |
| 2011 | Outside traffickers move in. Tomás negotiates "peace for a tithe" — the fatal compromise. He stops being called Tomás. |
| 2012 | La Cosecha ("The Harvest") named at a candlelit sermon. EL SERENO speaks for the first time as a title, not a man. |
| 2015 | Esteban publicly breaks with Sereno; the chapel becomes a quiet resistance waypoint. |
| 2016 | Cartel del Faro takes Meridian's docks; the Union is hollowed out from inside (7, "Union Dues"). |
| 2017 | Tobias Voss, discharged couriers' mate, takes odd smuggling runs to pay Ren's legal debts. |
| 2019 | Deputy Mayor SILAS CRANE funds "Task Force Limpio" — officially anti-cartel, actually the cleanup crew. |
| 2020 | TOBIAS steals THE LEDGER from the Molino Viejo vault during a hurricane party. Flees. |
| 2021 | El Sereno's bounty circulates quietly across three seas. Los Cobradores form (26.2). |
| 2022 | Ren Voss washes out of maritime interdiction after refusing a corrupt order (7 opening). |
| 2023 | The voicemail reaches Ren. THE GAME BEGINS. |

# SECTION 65 — FACTION DOSSIERS (uniforms, behavior, sound)

| Faction | Colors / Silhouette (42.3) | Behavior (6.4) | Sound ID | Territory |
|---------|---------------------------|----------------|----------|-----------|
| LA COSECHA (cult) | Tall angular hoods, long ochre coats, candle badges | Fight to the death; prayer pauses before ambush | sfx.fac.cosecha.* | Isla Sombra |
| CARTEL DEL FARO | Slick sportswear, teal/white, fast profiles | Morale breaks at losses; will flee and regroup | sfx.fac.cartel.* | Docks, highway, refinery |
| MERIDIAN PD + LIMPIO | Boxed navy, riot white for Limpio | Procedure first, overwhelming force at 3+ heat | sfx.fac.pd.* | City districts |
| THE PEOPLE | Mixed civilians, market cloth, murals | Support Ren with tips, hideouts, cheers (26.4) | sfx.fac.pueblo.* | Alto Barro, beaches |
- Sub-classes per faction: GRUNT (HP 100), BRUISER (HP 160, shotgun, suppresses), OFFICER (HP 130, buffs morale within 12 m, radio-calls reinforcements).
- Faction hostility matrix lives in /data/factions.json (Section 10.4).

# SECTION 73 — STORY MISSIONS (11) + STRONGHOLDS (3)

--- M01 "LANDING" (Isla Sombra, tutorial, ~15min) ---
B1 Wake in storm surf — voicemail plays whole (cold open, 55.2 seed). | CP
B2 Walk beach: movement tutorial diegetic (Poncho paw-prints in sand lead you). | CP
B3 Find Poncho in poacher trap — FREE HIM (24 origin). | CP  [F: none, unhurtable dog]
B4 Reach Aldea del Faro; Viuda gives the world's softest quest: "bring me 3 herbs."
B5 Herbs teach crouch + gather + the island's beauty (golden hour 43.2). | CP
B6 First noise: cult tax collectors shake a fisherman. CHOICE: intervene loud / tail them (stealth taster). | CP
B7 Pistol "Culebra" found in Tobias's buried toolbox (cache tutorial) + first Sermon Tape tease. | CP
B8 Radio: Camila Sol mentions the lighthouse cult summit. Chapter card. END.
DIALOGUE DRAFT — Viuda: "Your brother left debts and footprints. The footprints I liked."
FAIL STATES: none harsh; patience unlimited (kindness 35).

--- M02 "SIGNAL" (Cresta Sombría, mast 1, Moment 1) ---
B1 Climb tutorial: ledge/vault/zipline (6.1 full pass). | CP
B2 Approach Mast 1 — dusk; plan route (tag tutorial with U01). | CP
B3 Clear base (3 grunts + 1 bruiser) any style (6.3 loud/stealth both valid). | CP
B4 CLIMB MAST — 52#1 FIRST MAST SUNRISE fires on summit (FOG BURN, theme, map unfurl). | CP
B5 Descend via zipline (joy ride mandated 42.1) → Poncho digs cache A6 (fetch teach).
B6 Sereno's first radio sermon (Sermon Tape 01 auto-plays under). END.
DRAFT — Sereno tape 1: "They call it a harvest because we share it. Every hand eats. Ask the priest: charity failed. We succeeded."

--- M03 "THE LEDGER'S TRAIL" (Aldea + Selva, ~20min) ---
B1 Viuda decodes Tobias's crate marks: two leads (mill, chapel). | CP
B2 CHAPEL beat: Padre Esteban, quiet lore room (47.1 3-read), prayer optional (never mocked). | CP
B3 MILK RUN: 2 herbal caches + teach crafting (72 V2). | CP
B4 Infiltrate Puerto Chico "La Aduana" (Outpost 01 = mission set): find smuggler's ledger page 1/3. | CP
B5 Escape cutters via Panga boat (boat tutorial 6.6). | CP
B6 O09 cult robes schematic unlocked (disguise tease for M10). END.
DRAFT — Marisol (radio): "Tobi used to say the sea only keeps what it wants. The sea wants that book, Ren."

--- M04 "SERMON" (Molino Viejo approach + veranda, Moment 2) ---
B1 Truck tail to Molino mill district; overwatch tutorial (W10 fixed emplacement teaser). | CP
B2 Cult gathering: Sereno's candlelit sermon LIVE (crowd-sim 30 peds, hymn 51.1 live). | CP
B3 YOU ARE NOTICED — "El Sereno will see you now." Escorted (weapon holstered by force — drama). | CP
B4 52#2 TEA WITH SERENO (4 min, no weapon, gaze-acting 44.3, hymn humming). Choice lines: [Mock him] [Ask for Tobias] [Say nothing] — all continue, all recorded by 26.4. | CP
B5 Forced exit at gunpoint to the cliff road — TRAVERSAL ESCAPE (fall, swim, zipline, gone). | CP
B6 He lets you go. "Bring me my book." The tea cup memento ghosted (49 foreshadow). END.
DRAFT — Sereno: "You mistake me for a violent man. Violence is a vocabulary for those who cannot read. Sit. Drink. It's only tea."

--- M05 "CROSSING" (sea road, act bridge, ~10min) ---
B1 Beto's ferry at dawn; montage-lite sail (cinematic 55: 3 shots, no loading). | CP
B2 Cabin rest scene: Poncho seasick (comedy beat 44.6 budget), Ren's journal (story-so-far toggle). | CP
B3 Meridian skyline reveal (postcard law 42.2 — cyan/magenta 43.1). | CP
B4 Hands meetup at Alto Barro: heist tease, phone mechanics intro (jobs 30 later). | CP
B5 First city drive free (V01 gift from Marisol's cousin). Free-roam unlocks. END.

--- M06 "GILT INVITATION" (Downtown bank, ~25min) ---
B1 Recon the Gilt Kings' bank facade with camera mini-quest (photo 28 side-teach). | CP
B2 Meet "LUCKY" for the first time AS A TOURIST (54/26.2 plant; only later players scream). | CP
B3 Outfit O06 (Gala) + social stealth beat: talk past lobby (dialogue tree small, all roads work). | CP
B4 Vault mini-game: U03 hack + timed drill (31 minigame rules). | CP
B5 ALARM — escape the tower: stairwell brawl OR window zip to parking garage. | CP
B6 Getaway setpiece: 2-star chase (heat taste, failable-forward: caught = money loss only, story continues). | CP
B7 Ledger page 2/3 + Gilt Kings name CRANE's Limpio. END.
DRAFT — Hands: "The bank is a temple, Ren. And like all temples: the real god is in the basement."

--- M07 "UNION DUES" (Old Harbor, ~25min) ---
B1 Dock infiltration (Cartel turf): crane stealth playground (vertical 42.3 silhouettes). | CP
B2 Optional: free 3 dockworkers (THE PEOPLE +5% each, 26.1). | CP
B3 W07 "Festival" setpiece crate: the game WANTS you to try it here (suppression tutorial F07 tease). | CP
B4 Doña Marea's gunship arrival tease (roar, searchlights — boss breadcrumb). | CP
B5 Convoy escape: steal V03 and run the harbor highway (first vehicle combat). | CP
B6 Ledger page 3/3 — LEDGER COMPLETE: names, boats, the trade date. END.

--- M08 "LIMPIO" (city-wide, Moment 3 prep, ~30min) ---
B1 ELD edito cracks Limpio comms; Task Force ambushes the loft — SAFEHOUSE DEFENSE (first base fight, 49 stakes). | CP
B2 Escape split: Ren vs Poncho carrying scrapbook (PONCHO SOLO 90s: he hides in print press — later beloved segment). | CP
B3 Regroup at Teatro Alba (poster egg 54.2 in frame — do it). | CP
B4 Plan the broadcast with Camila + Aurelio. V11 "Obelisco" fight tease (El Limpiador voice on loudspeakers). | CP
B5 STREET WAR: 4-star escape to Aerodromo (or rail yard) — Moment-adjacent scale. | CP
B6 Survive; Crane swears cleanup. Ren decides: BROADCAST. END.

--- M09 "BROADCAST" (Meridian TV + all radios, 52#3 MOMENT) ---
B1 Two-path entry: rooftop antennas (stealth, rain) OR front door (loud, setpiece). | CP
B2 Station floors: 3 arenas, mixed PD + cartel (they shoot EACH OTHER — war 26.1 live). | CP
B3 Rehearsal booth beat: rewrite the speech (choice of tone sets 26.4 colors — script 74). | CP
B4 52#3 THE CITY APOLOGY — broadcast fires (crowds stop, cars idle, 3 stations sync). | CP

--- B1 LA SARGENTO (Molino courtyard, rain) ---
P1 "FORMAR": shield-wall 4 grunts on cadence; drums telegraph | break drum = stagger 4s window

P1 "TIEMPO 0:00": 4 bomb-columns activate; clock-hand platforms start (45-s cycle); he kites
P2 "SEGUNDO MANO": sniping from pendulum chamber; bomb timers 45s→35s; cutting his zip lines drops him 3s
P3 "CUARTO DE HORA": hands run backwards; arena rotates; one safe wedge; final charge
DOWN LINE: "Synchronized... at last." (cracked watch memento 49)
--- B3 DOÑA MAREA (harbor chase → pier) ---
P1 GUNSHIP RUNS x3: harpoon fuel lines (aim windows 4s), her taunts on loudhailer
P2 PIER BRAWL: she fights with anchor chain (sweep 3m), boats explode around (48 chains)
P3 TIDE RISING: pier shrinks; one harpoon left; the sunset (43.2 MUST hit)
DOWN LINE: "The water... keeps what it wants." (echo of Marisol M3 — intended)
--- B4 EL FRAILE (bell tower) ---
P1 SILENT: big-swing telegraphs by BELL TONE (33 accessibility by design); parry windows on 3rd ring
P2 TOLL: bells automated + smoke; he listens — noise decoy tools shine (T03, whistle S08)
P3 LAST BELL: chain wrap; mercy option [subdue] vs [strike] (26.4 points)
DOWN: he kneels, rings once, softly. No line. (respect 34.3)
--- B5 EL LIMPIADOR (Downtown night) ---
P1 VAN PHALANX: V11s reposition cover; EMP node U03 hacks to stall 6s
P2 LIMPIO WAVES: white-armored elites + tear gas (cough anims, no blur if photosens)
P3 CRANE: rifle + riot shield; break glass atrium for verticals
DOWN LINE: "Report... the city is clean..." (delusional to the end — banality)
--- F EL SERENO (lighthouse, monsoon) — 3 phases (25 finale spec) ---
P1 THE GATHERING: cult waves vs your garrisons; he watches from gallery, HYMN live (51.1)
P2 THE HOST: cane blade duel — precise, polite, calls your moves ("Good. Again.")

  [Tobias]: "Then the family was always the ledger." / [Ledger]: "Thank you. It was only ever paper." / [Third way]: "Ah. You were listening."

# SECTION 76 — SERMON TAPES (12) — El Sereno (23.4 law: steelman, never strawman)
T01 "THE TITHE": charity failed; we feed the hill; ask the priest who ate.
T02 "ORDER AND BREAD": chaos starves children; discipline feeds them; discuss.
T03 "THE MILL": what the mill owed us, we now collect; memory is accounting.
T04 "ON VIOLENCE": violence is a vocabulary for the illiterate; I read fluently.
T05 "THE HARBOR QUEEN": Ines thinks water owns her; she will learn tithe, too.
T06 "CAVES AND FAITH": the island kept secrets before priests; we only rent them.
T07 "THE COURIER'S BROTHER": loyalty to blood is the last virtue; admire it. (chills)
T08 "ON THE MARCH": when they come with their book, offer tea; the rest is mine.
T09 "THE RECKONING": names in a ledger are prayers of a kind; some get answered.
T10 "LA REINA'S CHILDREN": after the hurricane, we were neighbors; remember that.
T11 "WHAT I OWE": I did the math; I owe everyone; therefore I collect. Forgive me.
T12 "THE LAST LIGHT": at the faro, we end the harvest. Everyone eats. Everyone.
NOTE (direction): each tape 45-70s; hymn underneath at -18dB; NO stingers; let silence end them.

# SECTION 77 — TOBIAS TAPES (8) — the brother (heart of the game, 47.3)
V01 "FIRST MESSAGE": Ren, if this reaches you, don't come. (laughs) I know you. Come.
V02 "MILL NIGHT": the book's real; the names are worse; laughing to not scream.
V03 "SEA SHANTY": drunken cover of Ren's Theme (51.2 radio law payoff) + apology.
V04 "THE FERRYMAN'S DEBT": Beto carried me; Viuda hid me; the island is kind.
V05 "WHAT I DID": confession — he took the book to burn it; couldn't; hoped Ren would.
V06 "CAVE CAMP": hums Poncho's motif badly; asks if Ren got a dog yet. (devastating)
V07 "DARK": they found the shore road; if I stop talking, I stopped choosing to.
V08 "FOR THE TRADE" (M10 end, plays whole): whatever he asks — I'd do it again.
NOTE: audio logs diegetic (wind-up player); full transcript in journal; no jump-scares.

# SECTION 82 — CORE ALGORITHMS (implement per pseudocode)

ENEMY FSM (6.4): states PATROL,SUSPICIOUS,SEARCH,COMBAT,FLEE,DEAD,DOWNED.
  on_perception(event): meter += weight(dist,light,stance,noise); SUSPICIOUS at 0.35; COMBAT at 0.8 or ally_call.
  SEARCH: sweep LKP (6.3) grid 3m; 2 probes then flank pick from cover_graph; morale<0.2 -> FLEE (faction matrix).
  reinforce(): nearest garrison waves 2 max (26.1) — route spawn_points, 25m min.
TRAFFIC GRAPH: nodes=intersections, edges=lanes(m); cars target speed=min(lane,curve); gap rule: keep 1.5s; panic impulse => scatter targets off-graph 8s then rejoin.
PEDESTRIAN GRAPH: sidewalks with interest nodes (shop,cafe,view); panic->nearest door; night weight to venues (46.1).
WAVES (45.1): H(p,t)=sum Ai*sin(wi*t+phi(p)); 3 Gerstner waves LOD by camera height (45.4).
CONTRACT SEED: seed=int(date.strftime('%Y%m%d'))*7+archetype_id; RNG=mulberry32(seed) (30 daily law: same day = same contract).
CITY GRAMMAR (4.2): blocks=grammar rules: [Ave]+[Block(g,w)] where g in {grid,radial,terrace}; hero blocks hand-pinned 30 (4.2); heights by district table; Alto Barro: terrace(min_lot 4x6m, stair 18%).
DETECTION METER (6.3): meter rate = base/(dist^2 * light * stance); decay 0.2/s; team share at 8m radius (shouts).
